Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The extremal operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 145. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 996 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the extremal space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 145 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 996. QED.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^591(S, F) is trivial. QED.
